HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_09f4c9e339081f6d12e6","text":"2. **The repeated participle stabilizes a role/object mismatch.** Fixed **أَنتُمْ عَٰبِدُونَ** is a nominal predication, while **أَعْبُدُ** remains verbal; repetition now adds textual duration to that grammatical contrast. `ع ب د B003` supplies submissive service, and `ع ب د B006` supplies the honor accorded to what is served. The addressees’ role is structurally held apart from the speaker’s continuing object-relation. I infer that a role repeated unchanged across the intervening reverse clause is being presented as stable. The reading shifts from failure to perform one act to an enduring mismatch in allegiance and recognition.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B001","root_000973/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_1544bbb45b85df7af0cc","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"109:5:3\",\"root_000973\",[\"B001\",\"B004\"]],[\"109:5:5\",\"root_000973\",[\"B001\",\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Son ayrım öncesindeki bu tekrar, hiçbir tarafın ötekinin kulluk düzenine ait veya onun sahipliği altında sayılmamasını sağlayan son tasnif işlevi görür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B010"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_1a4b003fe8129496121a","text":"3. **Resistance to scattering becomes stronger after repetition.** ع ب د B010, \"scattering in different directions,\" is activated by the plural and by the copied 109:3 structure. Mechanism: the exact repetition gathers possible scattered divergences into one formal statement; individual variations do not matter for the packet's declared boundary. The abductive move is mine: the packet gives the scattering branch; I infer that duplicated syntax suppresses dispersion. Reading change: 109:5 reads as a second clamp on the group-level boundary.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_4777c3236bdabeb1d5ca","text":"The final ayah, when reread backward, made 109:5 feel immediately transitional. At first it could look like duplication of 109:3, but 109:6 shows why it returns: the repeated denial is the last sorting act before dīn is distributed by pronoun. The intervening 109:4 also made the repetition non-redundant, because the addressees' role-denial is restated after the speaker has refused relation to their past worship. This strengthened ع ب د B001/B004 as status-protection: no party is counted as belonging to the other's worship-order.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_51040fc762fd8e475b18","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"109:5:3\",\"root_000973\",[\"B003\"]],[\"109:5:5\",\"root_000973\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"109:3'ün aynen dönmesi, aradaki ters yönlü sınamadan sonra iki yöndeki ibadet özdeşleşmesinin de kapanmış olduğunu mühürler.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000504/B001","root_000973/B010"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_577d773da8f99896417b","text":"109:6 was the decisive later activator. Its immediate **لَكُمْ / وَلِيَ** partition changed the repeated clause from a backward-looking echo into the threshold of explicit allocation, and it supplied the directional mechanism needed for the exploratory `ع ب د B010` image of diverging routes. Its `د ي ن B001/B005` branches also confirmed that the stabilized mismatch concerns organized, customary allegiance rather than one omitted act.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B003","root_000973/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_70aaa692e545e9fcf310","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"109:5:3\",\"root_000973\",[\"B003\",\"B006\"]],[\"109:5:5\",\"root_000973\",[\"B003\",\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Tekrarlanan isim biçimi, muhatapların rolü ile konuşanın sürekli kulluk yönelimi arasındaki değer verme ve bağlılık uyuşmazlığını kalıcılaştırır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B010"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_9bd15310c59dbc98fc6f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"109:5:3\",\"root_000973\",[\"B010\"]],[\"109:5:5\",\"root_000973\",[\"B010\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Farklı kulluk nesnelerinin iki yolu birbirinden ayırdığı okuması, dağılma çağrışımının doğrudan olmayan kullanımına dayandığından spekülatiftir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_a287b7263e75c7b668ed","text":"1. **Exact repetition turns relational denial into closure.** The fixed ayah again supplies ع ب د B003 through عَٰبِدُونَ and أَعْبُدُ. The structural activator is its exact match with 109:3 after the intervening 109:4. Mechanism: the repeated line closes the loop: even after the speaker's own identity has been protected from their past practice, the addressees remain non-participants in the speaker's worship-relation. The abductive move is mine: the packet gives exact repetition; I infer closure from return-after-interruption. Reading change: 109:5 reads as sealing, not redundancy.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_a460c64cbfd958fbbc8c","text":"2. **The addressees are not servants in the speaker's honoring order.** ع ب د B006, \"honoring and magnifying,\" is activated by the same مَا أَعْبُدُ object and by the basmalah's named divine frame. Mechanism: the repeated plural participle denies not only ritual performance but a service/honor orientation. The abductive move is mine: the packet gives honor/service and repeated object orientation; I infer that repetition confirms a stable honor mismatch. Reading change: the ayah says that the speaker's object of worship is not merely unchosen by them but not honored by them in the required mode.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B010"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_ab656bc9e16c75cf5be0","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"109:5:3\",\"root_000973\",[\"B010\"]],[\"109:5:5\",\"root_000973\",[\"B010\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Tekrarlanan çoğul yapının muhataplar arasındaki bütün dağınık istisnaları kapattığı okuması da aynı uzak çağrışımı dil bilgisel kesinliğe dönüştürdüğü için spekülatiftir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B001","root_000973/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_cf94a5f6c8aa0069dcb3","text":"4. **The ayah prepares the final legal/accounting allotment.** ع ب د B001, \"slavery and owned status,\" and B004, \"making someone a slave,\" are activated by د ي ن B004 in 109:6. Mechanism: by denying that the addressees are worshippers of what the speaker worships, the ayah prevents their being counted as belonging to the speaker's obedience/order. The abductive move is mine: the packet gives servile status under ع ب د and subjugation/possession under د ي ن; I infer that final allotment depends on non-ownership/non-belonging. Reading change: the repeated denial becomes preparatory sorting for لَكُمْ دِينُكُمْ وَلِىَ دِينِ.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_dbf45eef172a29840e0b","text":"1. **Exact recurrence closes the reciprocal test.** The fixed clause repeats 109:3 word for word, again grounding both sides in `ع ب د B003`. Between the two occurrences, 109:4 tests the reverse relation with the speaker as participial servant and the addressees’ service in the perfect. The second occurrence is assigned the role of restoring the first orientation after that reversal. I infer from exact recurrence that neither direction of assimilation has survived the test. The line therefore functions as closure, not redundant restatement.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B010"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_f3c7f36d065663d0aa39","text":"3. **The service relation opens into divergent routes.** Exploratorily, fixed **عبد** can activate `ع ب د B010`, whose accepted image is groups or routes scattering in different directions. Its direct forms are more remote from **عَٰبِدُونَ**, so the branch is retained only because 109:6 immediately partitions **دِين** with **لَكُمْ / وَلِيَ**, while 109:3–5 have already separated service objects. The branch supplies spatial divergence; I infer the arrow that different service objects generate different ways. The fixed denial can thus be read as the final point at which the two routes cease to coincide before they are named as separate **دِين**.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
